Mathlib.Topology.Order.Cadlag

IsCadlagstructureMathlib.Topology.Order.Cadlag, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $X$ to elements of $Y$. Then we can say that $\mathrm{IsCadlag}$ holds for $f$.
Formula
$f \in X \rightarrow Y \vdash \mathrm{IsCadlag} (f)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $X$ à des éléments de $Y$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\mathrm{IsCadlag}$ tient pour $f$.

IsLeftContinuousdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Cadlag, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $X$ to elements of $Y$. Then $\mathrm{IsLeftContinuous}$ holds for $f$, if for all elements $a$ of $X$, $\mathrm{ContinuousWithinAt}$ holds for $f$, $\operatorname{Iio}$ applied to $a$ and $a$.
Formula
$f \in X \rightarrow Y \vdash \mathrm{IsLeftContinuous} (f):\Leftrightarrow \forall a \in X . \mathrm{ContinuousWithinAt} (f , \operatorname{Iio} (a), a)$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $X$ à des éléments de $Y$. Alors $\mathrm{IsLeftContinuous}$ tient pour $f$ si pour tous les éléments $a$ de $X$, $\mathrm{ContinuousWithinAt}$ tient pour $f$, $\operatorname{Iio}$ appliqué à $a$ et $a$.

Mathlib.Topology.Order.Category.AlexDisc

AlexDiscstructureMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.AlexDisc, type
Wording
Alexandrov disc (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
The Alexandrov discs are a basic type.
Formula
$\mathrm{AlexDisc} \ \mathsf{set}$
French
Les disques d'Alexandrov sont un type de base.
AlexDisc.Iso.mkdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.AlexDisc, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $e$ be a homeomorphism of the set of carriers of $\operatorname{toTopCat}$ applied to $x$ and the set of carriers of $\operatorname{toTopCat}$ applied to $x_{1}$. Then $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $e$ is an isomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{Homeomorph} (\operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{toTopCat} (x)), \operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{toTopCat} (x_{1})))\vdash \operatorname{mk} (e)\in \operatorname{Iso} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $e$ un homéomorphisme de l'ensemble d'ensembles sous-jacents de $\operatorname{toTopCat}$ appliqué à $x$ et l'ensemble d'ensembles sous-jacents de $\operatorname{toTopCat}$ appliqué à $x_{1}$. Alors $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $e$ est un isomorphisme de $x$ et $x_{1}$.
AlexDisc.ofdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.AlexDisc, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $X$ be a set. Then $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $X$ is an Alexandrov disc defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $X$.
Formula
$X \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{of} (X):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{of} (X))\in \mathrm{AlexDisc}$
French
Soit $X$ un ensemble. Alors $\operatorname{of}$ appliqué à $X$ est un disque d'Alexandrov défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{of}$ appliqué à $X$.
alexDiscEquivPreorddefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.AlexDisc, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
$\mathrm{alexDiscEquivPreord}$ is an equivalence of the set of Alexandrov discs and $\mathrm{Preord}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{alexDiscEquivPreord} \in \operatorname{Equivalence} (\mathrm{AlexDisc} , \mathrm{Preord})$
French
$\mathrm{alexDiscEquivPreord}$ est une équivalence de l'ensemble de disques d'Alexandrov et $\mathrm{Preord}$.

Mathlib.Topology.Order.Category.FrameAdjunction

Locale.PTdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.FrameAdjunction, type
Wording
no wording
English
Let $L$ be a set. Then an element of $\operatorname{PT}$ applied to $L$ is an element of the function that maps $L$ to the set of frame homomorphisms of $L$ and the set of propositions.
Formula
$L \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{PT} (L):= \lambda L . \mathrm{FrameHom} (L , \mathrm{Prop})$
French
Soit $L$ un ensemble. Alors un élément de $\operatorname{PT}$ appliqué à $L$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $L$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de repères de $L$ et l'ensemble de propositions.
Locale.adjunctionTopToLocalePTdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.FrameAdjunction, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
$\operatorname{adjunctionTopToLocalePT}$ is an adjunction of the top to locale and $\operatorname{pt}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{adjunctionTopToLocalePT} \in \operatorname{Adjunction} (\mathrm{topToLocale} , \operatorname{pt})$
French
$\operatorname{adjunctionTopToLocalePT}$ est une adjonction du plus grand élément à de la locale et $\operatorname{pt}$.
Locale.counitAppContdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.FrameAdjunction, function or constant
Wording
the counit of L (from the docstring)
English
Let $L$ be a locale. Then the counit of $L$ is a frame homomorphism of the set of carriers of the opposite of $L$ and the set of opens of $\operatorname{PT}$ applied to the set of carriers of the opposite of $L$.
Formula
$L \in \mathrm{Locale} \vdash \operatorname{counitAppCont} (L)\in \mathrm{FrameHom} (\operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{unop} (L)), \operatorname{Opens} (\operatorname{PT} (\operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{unop} (L)))))$
French
Soit $L$ une locale. Alors la coünité de $L$ est un homomorphisme de repères de l'ensemble d'ensembles sous-jacents de l'opposé de $L$ et l'ensemble d'ouverts de $\operatorname{PT}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ensembles sous-jacents de l'opposé de $L$.
Locale.localePointOfSpacePointdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.FrameAdjunction, function or constant
Wording
the locale point of X and x1 (from the name)
English
Let $X$ be a set. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $X$. Then the locale point of $X$ and $x_{1}$ is an element of $\operatorname{PT}$ applied to the set of opens of $X$.
Formula
$X \in \mathrm{Set} , x_{1} \in X \vdash \operatorname{localePointOfSpacePoint} (X , x_{1})\in \operatorname{PT} (\operatorname{Opens} (X))$
French
Soit $X$ un ensemble. Soit $x_{1}$ un élément de $X$. Alors le point de locales de $X$ et $x_{1}$ est un élément de $\operatorname{PT}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ouverts de $X$.
Locale.openOfElementHomdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Category.FrameAdjunction, function or constant
Wording
the open of L (from the name)
English
Let $L$ be a set. Then the open of $L$ is a frame homomorphism of $L$ and $\wp \operatorname{PT} (L)$.
Formula
$L \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{openOfElementHom} (L)\in \mathrm{FrameHom} (L , \wp \operatorname{PT} (L))$
French
Soit $L$ un ensemble. Alors l'ouvert de $L$ est un homomorphisme de repères de $L$ et $\wp \operatorname{PT} (L)$.

Wording
no wording
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then an element of $\operatorname{Fill}$ applied to $x$ is an element of the function that maps $x$ to $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{\mathrm{and} (\mathrm{if} (\operatorname{IsSuccPrelimit} (\ProdfstMACRoZ {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}), \mathrm{Leq} (0 , \ProdsndMACRoAA {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})})), \mathrm{if} (\operatorname{IsPredPrelimit} (\ProdfstMACRoZ {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}), \mathrm{Leq} (\ProdsndMACRoAA {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}, 0)))}$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{Fill} (x):= \lambda x . \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{\mathrm{and} (\mathrm{if} (\operatorname{IsSuccPrelimit} (\ProdfstMACRoAG {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}), \mathrm{Leq} (0 , \ProdsndMACRoAH {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})})), \mathrm{if} (\operatorname{IsPredPrelimit} (\ProdfstMACRoAG {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}), \mathrm{Leq} (\ProdsndMACRoAH {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}, 0)))}$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors un élément de $\operatorname{Fill}$ appliqué à $x$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $x$ à $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{\mathrm{and} (\mathrm{if} (\operatorname{IsSuccPrelimit} (\ProdfstMACRoZ {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}), \mathrm{Leq} (0 , \ProdsndMACRoAA {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})})), \mathrm{if} (\operatorname{IsPredPrelimit} (\ProdfstMACRoZ {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}), \mathrm{Leq} (\ProdsndMACRoAA {\operatorname{ofLex} (x_{2})}, 0)))}$.

Mathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic

ContinuousOrderHomstructureMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, type
Wording
continuous order homomorphism of x and x1 (from the name)
English
Let $x$ and $x_{1}$ be sets. Then the continuous order homomorphisms of $x$ and $x_{1}$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x , x_{1} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{1})\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soient $x$ et $x_{1}$ des ensembles. Alors les homomorphismes d'ordres continus de $x$ et $x_{1}$ sont un type de base.
ContinuousOrderHom.compdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous order homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Let $g$ be a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x_{1} , x_{2}), g \in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (f , g)\in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{2})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors la composition de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x_{2}$.
ContinuousOrderHom.copydefMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the copy of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $\mathrm{f'}$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x_{1}$ such that $\mathrm{f'}$ is equal to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the copy of $f$ and $\mathrm{f'}$ is a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{1}), \mathrm{f'} \in x \rightarrow x_{1} , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{f'}}{\operatorname{coe} (f)}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (f , \mathrm{f'} , h)\in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Soit $\mathrm{f'}$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x_{1}$ telle que $\mathrm{f'}$ soit égal à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors la copie de $f$ et $\mathrm{f'}$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x_{1}$.
ContinuousOrderHom.iddefMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the identity of x (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then the identity of $x$ is a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the identity function and $\mathrm{continuous\_id}$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{id} (x):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{id} , \mathrm{continuous\_id})\in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x)$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors l'identité de $x$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la fonction d'identités et $\mathrm{continuous\_id}$.
ContinuousOrderHom.toContinuousMapdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the continuous map of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the continuous map of $f$ is a continuous map of $x$ and $x_{1}$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $\operatorname{toOrderHom}$ applied to $f$ and $\mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom\_\_continuous\_toFun}$ applied to $f$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{toContinuousMap} (f):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{toFun} (\operatorname{toOrderHom} (f)), \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom\_\_continuous\_toFun} (f))\in \mathrm{ContinuousMap} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors l'application continue de $f$ est une application continue de $x$ et $x_{1}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toOrderHom}$ appliqué à $f$ et $\mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom\_\_continuous\_toFun}$ appliqué à $f$.
ContinuousOrderHomClassclassMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $F$ be a set. Let $x$ and $x_{1}$ be elements of $\mathrm{outParam}$ applied to the set of sets. Then we can say that $\mathrm{ContinuousOrderHomClass}$ holds for $F$, $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$F \in \mathrm{Set} , x , x_{1} \in \mathrm{outParam} (\mathrm{Set})\vdash \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHomClass} (F , x , x_{1})\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $F$ un ensemble. Soient $x$ et $x_{1}$ des éléments de $\mathrm{outParam}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ensembles. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\mathrm{ContinuousOrderHomClass}$ tient pour $F$, $x$ et $x_{1}$.
ContinuousOrderHomClass.toContinuousOrderHomdefMathlib.Topology.Order.Hom.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the continuous order homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an element of $F$. Then the continuous order homomorphism of $f$ is a continuous order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$f \in F \vdash \operatorname{toContinuousOrderHom} (f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousOrderHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un élément de $F$. Alors l'homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $f$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres continu de $x$ et $x_{1}$.

Mathlib.Topology.Partial

PContinuousdefMathlib.Topology.Partial, predicate
Wording
f is continuous (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function of $X$ and $Y$. Then $f$ is continuous, if for all elements $s$ of $\wp Y$, if $s$ is open, then the preimage of $f$ and $s$ is open.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{PFun} (X , Y)\vdash \mathrm{PContinuous} (f):\Leftrightarrow \forall s \in \wp Y . \mathrm{IsOpen} (s)\rightarrow \mathrm{IsOpen} (\operatorname{preimage} (f , s))$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction de $X$ et $Y$. Alors $f$ est continu si pour tous les éléments $s$ de $\wp Y$, si $s$ est ouvert, alors la préimage de $f$ et $s$ est ouverte.

Mathlib.Topology.PartialHomeomorph.Basic

PartialHomeomorph.homeomorphOfImageSubsetSourcedefMathlib.Topology.PartialHomeomorph.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the homeomorphism of e (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a partial homeomorphism of $X$ and $Y$ such that $s$ is less than or equal to the source of $\operatorname{toPartialEquiv}$ applied to $e$ and the image of $\operatorname{toFun'}$ applied to $e$ and $s$ is equal to $t$. Then the homeomorphism of $e$ is a homeomorphism of the set of elements of $s$ and the set of elements of $t$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{PartialHomeomorph} (X , Y), s \leq \operatorname{source} (\operatorname{toPartialEquiv} (e)), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{image} (\operatorname{toFun'} (e), s)}{t}\vdash \operatorname{homeomorphOfImageSubsetSource} (e , \mathrm{hs} , \mathrm{ht})\in \mathrm{Homeomorph} (\operatorname{Elem} (s), \operatorname{Elem} (t))$
French
Soit $e$ un homéomorphisme partiel de $X$ et $Y$ tel que $s$ soit inférieur ou égal à la source de $\operatorname{toPartialEquiv}$ appliqué à $e$ et l'image de $\operatorname{toFun'}$ appliqué à $e$ et $s$ soit égale à $t$. Alors l'homéomorphisme de $e$ est un homéomorphisme de l'ensemble d'éléments de $s$ et l'ensemble d'éléments de $t$.
PartialHomeomorph.ofContinuousOpendefMathlib.Topology.PartialHomeomorph.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the partial homeomorphism of e (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a partial equivalence of $X$ and $Y$ such that $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $e$ is continuous on the source of $e$, $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $e$ is an open map and the source of $e$ is open. Then the partial homeomorphism of $e$ is a partial homeomorphism of $X$ and $Y$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{PartialEquiv} (X , Y), \mathrm{ContinuousOn} (\operatorname{toFun} (e), \operatorname{source} (e)), \mathrm{IsOpenMap} (\operatorname{toFun} (e)), \mathrm{IsOpen} (\operatorname{source} (e))\vdash \operatorname{ofContinuousOpen} (e , \mathrm{hc} , \mathrm{ho} , \mathrm{hs})\in \mathrm{PartialHomeomorph} (X , Y)$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence partielle de $X$ et $Y$ telle que $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $e$ soit continu sur la source de $e$, $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $e$ soit une application ouverte et la source de $e$ soit ouverte. Alors l'homéomorphisme partiel de $e$ est un homéomorphisme partiel de $X$ et $Y$.
PartialHomeomorph.ofContinuousOpenRestrictdefMathlib.Topology.PartialHomeomorph.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the partial homeomorphism of e (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a partial equivalence of $X$ and $Y$ such that $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $e$ is continuous on the source of $e$ and $\operatorname{domRestrict}$ applied to $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $e$ is an open map. Then the partial homeomorphism of $e$ is a partial homeomorphism of $X$ and $Y$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{PartialEquiv} (X , Y), \mathrm{ContinuousOn} (\operatorname{toFun} (e), \operatorname{source} (e)), \mathrm{IsOpenMap} (\operatorname{domRestrict} (\operatorname{source} (e), \operatorname{toFun} (e)))\vdash \operatorname{ofContinuousOpenRestrict} (e , \mathrm{hc} , \mathrm{ho})\in \mathrm{PartialHomeomorph} (X , Y)$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence partielle de $X$ et $Y$ telle que $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $e$ soit continu sur la source de $e$ et $\operatorname{domRestrict}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $e$ soit une application ouverte. Alors l'homéomorphisme partiel de $e$ est un homéomorphisme partiel de $X$ et $Y$.

Wording
the partial homeomorphism of f and h (from the name)

Let $f$ be a partial equivalence of $X$ and $Y$ such that $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to the equivalence of $f$ is a homeomorphism. Then the partial homeomorphism of $f$ and $h$ is a partial homeomorphism of $X$ and $Y$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{PartialEquiv} (X , Y), \mathrm{IsHomeomorph} (\operatorname{coe} (\operatorname{toEquiv} (f)))\vdash \operatorname{ofIsHomeomorphToEquiv} (f , h)\in \mathrm{PartialHomeomorph} (X , Y)$
French
Soit $f$ une équivalence partielle de $X$ et $Y$ telle que $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à l'équivalence de $f$ soit un homéomorphisme. Alors l'homéomorphisme partiel de $f$ et $h$ est un homéomorphisme partiel de $X$ et $Y$.

Wording
the identity on the whole space of X (from the docstring)
English

Wording
the homeomorphism of e (from the name)
English

Then the homeomorphism of $e$ is a homeomorphism of $X$ and $Y$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{PartialHomeomorph} (X , Y), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{source} (\operatorname{toPartialEquiv} (e))}{\operatorname{univ}}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{target} (\operatorname{toPartialEquiv} (e))}{\operatorname{univ}}\vdash \operatorname{toHomeomorphOfSourceEqUnivTargetEqUniv} (e , h , \mathrm{h'})\in \mathrm{Homeomorph} (X , Y)$
French

Alors l'homéomorphisme de $e$ est un homéomorphisme de $X$ et $Y$.

BumpCovering.IsSubordinatedefMathlib.Topology.PartitionOfUnity, predicate
Wording
f is subordinate to U (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a bump covering of $X$ and $s$. Let $U$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $\wp X$. Then $f$ is subordinate to $U$, if for all elements $i$ of $x$, $\mathrm{tsupport}$ applied to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$ applied to $i$ is less than or equal to $U$ applied to $i$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{BumpCovering} (x , X , s), U \in x \rightarrow \wp X \vdash \operatorname{IsSubordinate} (f , U):\Leftrightarrow \forall i \in x . \mathrm{tsupport} (\operatorname{coe} (f (i)))\leq U (i)$
French
Soit $f$ un recouvrement de bosses de $X$ et $s$. Soit $U$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $\wp X$. Alors $f$ est subordonné à $U$ si pour tous les éléments $i$ de $x$, $\mathrm{tsupport}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$ appliqué à $i$ est inférieur ou égal à $U$ appliqué à $i$.

Wording
the sum of x6 and x7 (from the name)

Path.extenddefMathlib.Topology.Path, function or constant
Wording
the extension of x2 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a path of $x_{1}$ and $y$. Then the extension of $x_{2}$ is a continuous map of $\Real$ and $X$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \mathrm{Path} (x_{1} , y)\vdash \operatorname{extend} (x_{2})\in \mathrm{ContinuousMap} (\Real , X)$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un chemin de $x_{1}$ et $y$. Alors l'extension de $x_{2}$ est une application continue de $\Real$ et $X$.

Wording

English

Formula

French
L'identité est un chemin de $0$ et $1$.

Wording
the inverse of x2 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a path of $a$ and $b$. Then the inverse of $x_{2}$ is a path of $\operatorname{inv}$ applied to $a$ and $\operatorname{inv}$ applied to $b$ defined as the map of $x_{2}$ and $\mathrm{ContinuousInv\_\_continuous\_inv}$.
Formula

Path.mapdefMathlib.Topology.Path, function or constant
Wording
the map of x3 and h (from the name)
English

PreperfectdefMathlib.Topology.Perfect, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $C$ be an element of $\wp x$. Then $\mathrm{Preperfect}$ holds for $C$, if for all elements $x_{2}$ of $x$, if $x_{2}$ is an element of $C$, then $\mathrm{AccPt}$ holds for $x_{2}$ and the principal filter of $C$.
Formula
$C \in \wp x \vdash \mathrm{Preperfect} (C):\Leftrightarrow \forall x_{2} \in x . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {C}{x_{2}}\rightarrow \mathrm{AccPt} (x_{2} , \operatorname{principal} (C))$
French
Soit $C$ un élément de $\wp x$. Alors $\mathrm{Preperfect}$ tient pour $C$ si pour tous les éléments $x_{2}$ de $x$, si $x_{2}$ est un élément de $C$, alors $\mathrm{AccPt}$ tient pour $x_{2}$ et le filtre principal de $C$.

Mathlib.Topology.QuasiSeparated

IsQuasiSeparateddefMathlib.Topology.QuasiSeparated, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $s$ be an element of $\wp x$. Then $\mathrm{IsQuasiSeparated}$ holds for $s$, if for all elements $U$ and $V$ of $\wp x$, if $U \leq s$, then if $U$ is open, then if $U$ is compact, then if $V \leq s$, then if $V$ is open, then if $V$ is compact, then $\operatorname{inter}$ applied to $U$ and $V$ is compact.
Formula
$s \in \wp x \vdash \mathrm{IsQuasiSeparated} (s):\Leftrightarrow \forall U \in \wp x . \forall V \in \wp x . U \leq s \rightarrow \mathrm{IsOpen} (U)\rightarrow \mathrm{IsCompact} (U)\rightarrow V \leq s \rightarrow \mathrm{IsOpen} (V)\rightarrow \mathrm{IsCompact} (V)\rightarrow \mathrm{IsCompact} (\operatorname{inter} (U , V))$
French
Soit $s$ un élément de $\wp x$. Alors $\mathrm{IsQuasiSeparated}$ tient pour $s$ si pour tous les éléments $U$ et $V$ de $\wp x$, si $U \leq s$, alors si $U$ est ouvert, alors si $U$ est compact, alors si $V \leq s$, alors si $V$ est ouvert, alors si $V$ est compact, alors $\operatorname{inter}$ appliqué à $U$ et $V$ est compact.

SemicontinuousdefMathlib.Topology.Semicontinuity.Defs, predicate
Wording
r is semicontinuous (from the name)

SemicontinuousAtdefMathlib.Topology.Semicontinuity.Defs, predicate
Wording
r is semicontinuous at x3 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $r$ be a function from elements of $x$ and elements of $x_{1}$ to propositions. Let $x_{3}$ be an element of $x$. Then $r$ is semicontinuous at $x_{3}$, if for all elements $y$ of $x_{1}$, if $r$ holds for $x_{3}$ and $y$, then $\operatorname{Eventually}$ holds for the function that maps $\mathrm{x'}$ to $r$ applied to $\mathrm{x'}$ and $y$ and the neighbourhood filter of $x_{3}$.
Formula

French

IsSeparatedMapdefMathlib.Topology.SeparatedMap, predicate
Wording
f is a separated map (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $X$ to elements of $Y$. Then $f$ is a separated map, if for all elements $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ of $X$, if $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{1})}{f (x_{2})}$, then if $x_{1} \neq x_{2}$, then there exist elements $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$ of $\wp X$, such that $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$ are open, $x_{1}$ is an element of $x_{3}$, $x_{2}$ is an element of $x_{4}$ and $x_{3}$ is disjoint to $x_{4}$.
Formula
$f \in X \rightarrow Y \vdash \mathrm{IsSeparatedMap} (f):\Leftrightarrow \forall x_{1} \in X . \forall x_{2} \in X . \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{1})}{f (x_{2})}\rightarrow x_{1} \neq x_{2} \rightarrow (\exists x_{3} \in \wp X . \exists x_{4} \in \wp X . \mathrm{IsOpen} (x_{3})\wedge (\mathrm{IsOpen} (x_{4})\wedge (\MembershipmemMACRoAA {x_{3}}{x_{1}}\wedge (\MembershipmemMACRoAA {x_{4}}{x_{2}}\wedge \mathrm{Disjoint} (x_{3} , x_{4})))))$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $X$ à des éléments de $Y$. Alors $f$ est une application séparée si pour tous les éléments $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ de $X$, si $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{1})}{f (x_{2})}$, alors si $x_{1} \neq x_{2}$, alors il existent des éléments $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$ de $\wp X$ tel que $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$ soient ouverts, $x_{1}$ soit un élément de $x_{3}$, $x_{2}$ soit un élément de $x_{4}$ et $x_{3}$ soit disjoint à $x_{4}$.

T2Quotient.liftdefMathlib.Topology.Separation.Hausdorff, function or constant
Wording
the lift of hf and x2 (from the name)
English

$\mathrm{Continuous} (f), x_{2} \in \mathrm{T2Quotient} (X)\vdash \operatorname{lift} (\mathrm{hf} , x_{2}):= \operatorname{lift} (f , \operatorname{compatible} (\mathrm{hf}), x_{2})\in Y$
French
Supposons que $f$ est continu. Soit $x_{2}$ un quotient de $X$. Alors le relèvement de $\mathrm{hf}$ et $x_{2}$ est un élément de $Y$ défini comme $\operatorname{lift}$ appliqué à $f$, $\operatorname{compatible}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{hf}$ et $x_{2}$.

Mathlib.Topology.Separation.SeparatedNhds

HasSeparatingCoverdefMathlib.Topology.Separation.SeparatedNhds, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ be elements of $\wp X$. Then $\mathrm{HasSeparatingCover}$ holds for $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$, if there exists a function $u$ from natural numbers to elements of $\wp X$, such that $x_{1}$ is less than or equal to the union of the function that maps $n$ to $u$ applied to $n$ and for all natural numbers $n_{1}$, $u$ applied to $n_{1}$ is open and the closure of $u$ applied to $n_{1}$ is disjoint to $x_{2}$.
Formula
$x_{1} , x_{2} \in \wp X \vdash \mathrm{HasSeparatingCover} (x_{1} , x_{2}):\Leftrightarrow \exists u \in \Natural \rightarrow \wp X . x_{1} \leq \operatorname{iUnion} (\lambda n . u (n))\wedge (\forall n_{1} \in \Natural . \mathrm{IsOpen} (u (n_{1}))\wedge \mathrm{Disjoint} (\mathrm{closure} (u (n_{1})), x_{2}))$
French
Soient $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ des éléments de $\wp X$. Alors $\mathrm{HasSeparatingCover}$ tient pour $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ s'il existe une fonction $u$ des nombres naturels à des éléments de $\wp X$ tel que $x_{1}$ soit inférieur ou égal à l'union de la fonction qui envoie $n$ à $u$ appliqué à $n$ et pour tous des nombres naturels $n_{1}$, $u$ appliqué à $n_{1}$ soit ouvert et la clôture de $u$ appliqué à $n_{1}$ soit disjointe à $x_{2}$.
SeparatedNhdsdefMathlib.Topology.Separation.SeparatedNhds, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ be elements of $\wp X$. Then $\mathrm{SeparatedNhds}$ holds for $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$, if there exist elements $U$ and $V$ of $\wp X$, such that $U$ and $V$ are open, $x_{1} \leq U$, $x_{2} \leq V$ and $U$ is disjoint to $V$.
Formula
$x_{1} , x_{2} \in \wp X \vdash \mathrm{SeparatedNhds} (x_{1} , x_{2}):\Leftrightarrow \exists U \in \wp X . \exists V \in \wp X . \mathrm{IsOpen} (U)\wedge (\mathrm{IsOpen} (V)\wedge (x_{1} \leq U \wedge (x_{2} \leq V \wedge \mathrm{Disjoint} (U , V))))$
French
Soient $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ des éléments de $\wp X$. Alors $\mathrm{SeparatedNhds}$ tient pour $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ s'il existent des éléments $U$ et $V$ de $\wp X$ tel que $U$ et $V$ soient ouverts, $x_{1} \leq U$, $x_{2} \leq V$ et $U$ soit disjoint à $V$.

IsCompactOpenCovereddefMathlib.Topology.Sets.CompactOpenCovered, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements $i$ of $x$ and elements of $X$ applied to $i$ to elements of $S$. Let $U$ be an element of $\wp S$. Then $\mathrm{IsCompactOpenCovered}$ holds for $i$, $f$ and $U$, if there exists an element $s$ of $\wp x$, such that there exists an element $h$ of $\operatorname{Finite}$ applied to $s$, such that there exists a function $V$ from elements $i_{2}$ of $x$ and elements of $\mathrm{Proof}$ applied to $\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{i_{2}}$ to opens of $X$ applied to $i_{2}$, such that for all elements $i$ of $x$, for all elements $h_{1}$ of $\mathrm{Proof}$ applied to $\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{i}$, the carrier of $V$ applied to $i$ and $h_{1}$ is compact and the union of the function that maps $i_{1}$ to the union of the function that maps $h_{2}$ to the image of $i$ applied to $i_{1}$ and $V$ applied to $i_{1}$ and $h_{2}$ is equal to $f$.
Formula
$f \in x \rightarrow X (i)\rightarrow S , U \in \wp S \vdash \mathrm{IsCompactOpenCovered} (i , f , U):\Leftrightarrow \exists s \in \wp x . \exists h \in \operatorname{Finite} (s). \exists V \in x \rightarrow \mathrm{Proof} (\MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{i_{2}})\rightarrow \operatorname{Opens} (X (i_{2})). (\forall i \in x . \forall h_{1} \in \mathrm{Proof} (\MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{i}). \mathrm{IsCompact} (\operatorname{carrier} (V (i , h_{1}))))\wedge (\EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{iUnion} (\lambda i_{1} . \operatorname{iUnion} (\lambda h_{2} . \operatorname{image} (i (i_{1}), V (i_{1} , h_{2}))))}{f})$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments $i$ de $x$ et des éléments de $X$ appliqué à $i$ à des éléments de $S$. Soit $U$ un élément de $\wp S$. Alors $\mathrm{IsCompactOpenCovered}$ tient pour $i$, $f$ et $U$ s'il existe un élément $s$ de $\wp x$ tel que il existe un élément $h$ de $\operatorname{Finite}$ appliqué à $s$ tel que il existe une fonction $V$ des éléments $i_{2}$ de $x$ et des éléments de $\mathrm{Proof}$ appliqué à $\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{i_{2}}$ à des ouverts de $X$ appliqué à $i_{2}$ tel que pour tous des éléments $i$ de $x$, pour tous des éléments $h_{1}$ de $\mathrm{Proof}$ appliqué à $\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{i}$, l'ensemble sous-jacent de $V$ appliqué à $i$ et $h_{1}$ soit compact et l'union de la fonction qui envoie $i_{1}$ à l'union de la fonction qui envoie $h_{2}$ à l'image de $i$ appliqué à $i_{1}$ et $V$ appliqué à $i_{1}$ et $h_{2}$ soit égale à $f$.

Wording
u is a open cover (from the name)
English

French

Wording

English
Let $C$ be a set. Let $X$ be a topological category. Then a presheaf of $C$ and $X$ is an element of the function that maps $C$ and $X$ to the set of functors of the set of opposites of the set of opens of the set of carriers of $X$ and $C$.

Wording
the presheaf to top of X and T (from the name)
English
Let $X$ and $T$ be topological categories. Then the presheaf to top of $X$ and $T$ is a presheaf of the set of sets and $X$ defined as the composition of the opposite of the topological category of $X$ and the action of the Yoneda and $T$.
Formula
$X , T \in \mathrm{TopCat} \vdash \operatorname{presheafToTop} (X , T):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{op} (\operatorname{toTopCat} (X)), \operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{yoneda} , T))\in \operatorname{Presheaf} (\mathrm{Set} , X)$
French
Soient $X$ et $T$ des catégories topologiques. Alors le préfaisceau à du plus grand élément de $X$ et $T$ est un préfaisceau de l'ensemble d'ensembles et $X$ défini comme la composition de l'opposé de la catégorie topologique de $X$ et l'action de l'Yoneda et $T$.

Soit $X$ une catégorie topologique. Soit $T$ une fonction des ensembles sous-jacents de $X$ à des ensembles. Alors le préfaisceau à du type de $X$ et $T$ est un préfaisceau de l'ensemble d'ensembles et $X$.

Mathlib.Topology.Sheaves.Sheaf

TopCat.Presheaf.IsSheafdefMathlib.Topology.Sheaves.Sheaf, predicate
Wording
F is a sheaf (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a presheaf of $C$ and $X$. Then $F$ is a sheaf, if $\operatorname{IsSheaf}$ holds for the Grothendieck topology of the set of carriers of $X$ and $F$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Presheaf} (C , X)\vdash \operatorname{IsSheaf} (F):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsSheaf} (\operatorname{grothendieckTopology} (\operatorname{carrier} (X)), F)$
French

TopCat.SheafdefMathlib.Topology.Sheaves.Sheaf, type
Wording

English

$C \in \mathrm{Set} , X \in \mathrm{TopCat} \vdash \operatorname{Sheaf} (C , X):= \lambda C , X . \operatorname{Sheaf} (\operatorname{grothendieckTopology} (\operatorname{carrier} (X)), C)$

Wording
the intersection union pullback cone of U and V (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a sheaf of $C$ and $X$. Let $U$ and $V$ be opens of the set of carriers of $X$. Then the intersection union pullback cone of $U$ and $V$ is a pullback cone of the map of the object of $F$ and the opposite in the homomorphism of $\mathrm{inf\_le\_left}$ and the map of the object of $F$ and the opposite in the homomorphism of $\mathrm{inf\_le\_right}$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Sheaf} (C , X), U , V \in \operatorname{Opens} (\operatorname{carrier} (X))\vdash \operatorname{interUnionPullbackCone} (F , U , V)\in \operatorname{PullbackCone} (\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{obj} (F), \operatorname{op} (\operatorname{homOfLE} (\mathrm{inf\_le\_left}))), \operatorname{map} (\operatorname{obj} (F), \operatorname{op} (\operatorname{homOfLE} (\mathrm{inf\_le\_right}))))$
French
Soit $F$ un faisceau de $C$ et $X$. Soient $U$ et $V$ des ouverts de l'ensemble d'ensembles sous-jacents de $X$. Alors le cône de produits fibrés d'unions d'intersections de $U$ et $V$ est un cône de produits fibrés de l'application de l'objet de $F$ et l'opposé dans l'homomorphisme de $\mathrm{inf\_le\_left}$ et l'application de l'objet de $F$ et l'opposé dans l'homomorphisme de $\mathrm{inf\_le\_right}$.

Wording
the intersection union pullback cone lift of V and s (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a sheaf of $C$ and $X$. Let $U$ and $V$ be opens of the set of carriers of $X$. Let $s$ be a pullback cone of the map of the object of $F$ and the opposite in the homomorphism of $\mathrm{inf\_le\_left}$ and the map of the object of $F$ and the opposite in the homomorphism of $\mathrm{inf\_le\_right}$. Then the intersection union pullback cone lift of $V$ and $s$ is a homomorphism of $\operatorname{pt}$ applied to $s$ and the action of the object of $F$ and $\operatorname{op}$ applied to $\MaxmaxMACRoAF {U}{V}$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Sheaf} (C , X), U , V \in \operatorname{Opens} (\operatorname{carrier} (X)), s \in \operatorname{PullbackCone} (\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{obj} (F), \operatorname{op} (\operatorname{homOfLE} (\mathrm{inf\_le\_left}))), \operatorname{map} (\operatorname{obj} (F), \operatorname{op} (\operatorname{homOfLE} (\mathrm{inf\_le\_right}))))\vdash \operatorname{interUnionPullbackConeLift} (F , U , V , s)\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{pt} (s), \operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{obj} (F), \operatorname{op} (\MaxmaxMACRoAM {U}{V})))$

English
Let $F$ be a sheaf of $C$ and $X$. Let $U$ and $V$ be opens of the set of carriers of $X$ such that $\MinminMACRoAG {U}{V}$ is equal to the bottom. Then $\operatorname{isProductOfDisjoint}$ applied to $F$, $U$, $V$ and $h$ is an element of $\operatorname{IsLimit}$ applied to $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the map of the object of $F$ and the opposite in the homomorphism of $\mathrm{le\_sup\_left}$ and the map of the object of $F$ and the opposite in the homomorphism of $\mathrm{le\_sup\_right}$.
Formula
